Use before writing ANY GraphQL mutation against a connected Shopify store — never guess mutation or input type names. Execution always goes through the store's connected Shopify tools; store building flows belong to shopify-builder, single-product listing SOP to shopify-product-launch. This skill is the pre-write signature dictionary and introspection guide only.
